- **RUNBOOK**: 1. Read the rules: `ravi specs get cli/agents --mode rules --json`. 2. Reproduce the failing call with `--json` and read `error.code` first; the code, not the message, is the branch point. 3. Exit `1` + `AGENT_NOT_FOUND`: read `error.suggestions` — real agent ids and names similar to what was asked. Retry with one of them, or list with `ravi agents list --json`. 4. Exit `2`: read `error.acceptedFl ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
- **RUNBOOK**: 1. `ravi agents list --json` — list agents visible to the current principal. 2. `ravi agents show &lt;id&gt; --json` — inspect one agent, including effective model fields (`effectiveProvider`, `effectiveModel`, `modelSource`, `modelPresetId`, `modelPresetVersion`). ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
- **RUNBOOK**: - `state = active` with a `last_error`: the watcher could not finish the task or inform the session; it retries every 30 s. - `state = delivered`: the result reached the session and the Console acknowledgement is pending. - No login (`ravi whoami`): the watcher skips until `ravi login` again. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cloud/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★11)
